Algorithm plan:
1. Define helper functions top-level no closures/lambdas.

4. Precompute FFT size for sequence length.
5. Smooth mirror descent function:
   - Inputs a, time_limit, beta or temp, n_iter_approx, rng, verbose, restart.
   - Works in sum=1 normalized, nonnegative.
   - Use target continuous M? For weights: p = conv(a). max_p. p_min? We choose weights = exp((p - max_p)/t). t = scale * (max_p - quantile_p) maybe. Need scale time.
   - Since p is nonnegative and length 2n-1. We want weights sum1. For temperature in units of p. Set t = temp_ratio * max_p, e.g. 0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0. If t small, focus top. For low, t=max_p (flat-ish). Because p max ~0.0002, t=0.05*max=1e-5.
   - To avoid underflow, subtract max, divide by t; exp; sum.
   - Gradient h = 2 * corr(w,a). We can use normalized h by dividing by its scale for step.
   - Update q=(h - min_h)/(max_h - min_h). Then log_a = log(a) - step * q. Normalize. This reduces high h; min unchanged. But if min_h are zeros? It not increase low ones relative except normalization. Could also subtract mean to increase low. Maybe use q=(h - mean_h)/range, with exponent clip. If h above mean decrease, below mean increase. Range = p99-p1? Choose step (log ratio) e.g. 0.2. Let's do centered: c = np.dot(a,h) (since sum a=1); range = max(h - c, 1e-15) maybe. log_a = log(a) - step * (h - c) / range. Then high >c decrease, low increase; total preserve? The exponent mean not zero due weighting by a, but normalize. Step small (0.1-1.0). Range ~O(1/n), so step/range large? Actually (h-c)/range O(1), log changes O(step). Good. Compute c = dot(a,h). Range = max(h) - c or p99. Use robust range = max(np.percentile(h,99)-np.percentile(h,1), 1e-15). But percentile each iter cost? Use max-min maybe extreme. We can use c and absmax = max(abs(h-c)); exponent = -step * (h-c)/absmax. Clip to [-3,3]. This gives relative change up to e^step. Good.
   - Since all positive? a may become zeros due underflow if step large. Clip min 1e-300? But with log_a min -? If a has zeros initially, log zero issues. We can keep small floor: a = max(a, eps/tiny_sum). Use a_safe = a + tiny. Or only positive shapes no zeros. We can allow zeros but log(0) -> -inf. Use log(max(a, 1e-300)).
   - Need maintain sum1. After update, a_new sum; if not finite, keep old.
   - Compute objective C_new = max(conv(a_new)); track best.
   - Use Nesterov/exponential? Maybe simple repeated updates enough.
   - Adaptive step: start step=0.2, if objective improves, maybe increase slightly; if stalls, decrease or perturb.
   - Restart: after many stalls, perturb a by moving mass from high a to low or add noise, or use best with random Gaussian in log.

Actually softmax parameterization with Adam: a_j ∝ exp(θ_j). Since scaling invariant, subtract max/mean each step to maintain. Gradient q = (h-c)/range. This is dimensionless. Adam with lr=0.1 maybe. This may be better than fixed small. We can implement without closures.
Pseudo:
   loga = log(a)+eps
   m=0; v=0
   for it:
      p, w, h; c=a.dot(h); q=(h-c)/range
      m = β1*m + (1-β1)*q
      v = β2*v + (1-β2)*q*q
      bias; z = mhat / (vhat+eps)
      step = lr * np.clip(z, -maxstep, maxstep)
      loga = loga - step  (maybe with Nesterov momentum?)
      a = exp(loga - loga.mean()); normalize
      track objective
      if stall: restart/perturb, adjust mgr
This "Adam" often fast. Need ensure positivity; loga finite. Values can drift; center loga each iter after normalization? If a=softmax(loga), we can set loga = log(after) to avoid drift. Detailed adam iteration:
   Compute p via conv(a)
   Compute h from weights for current power
   c = np.dot(a, h)
   maxdev = np.max(np.abs(h-c))
   if maxdev > eps:
      q = (h-c)/maxdev; q = np.clip(q,-5,5)
   else q=0
   m = b1*m + (1-b1)*q
   v = b2*v + (1-b2)*(q*q)
   mhat = m/(1-b1**t)
   vhat = v/(1-b2**t)
   step = lr * mhat / (sqrt(vhat)+eps)
   step = np.clip(step, -max_step, max_step)
   loga = loga - step
   loga -= loga.mean()? For invariance, subtract max for exp. `a_new = np.exp(loga - loga.max())`; `a_new /= a_new.sum()`. If nonfinite, fallback a.
   Recompute p_new, C_new. But note if we recomputed p, that's next p. We need C_new.
   If C_new < best_C: best.
   If C_new > max(C_current, best_C) * (1 + 0.2): maybe too bad; revert? Could happen. Add safeguard: if C_new > best_C * 1.5 or not finite, revert loga=a and reduce lr. But allowing soft objective increases can help. Use if C_new > best_C*2 or C_new > 1e-3? ab.
   For current_C, if C_new < current_C or (soft?), accept. Maybe always.
   lr adaptation: if C_new < current_C - 1e-15: lr = min(1.5, lr*1.02) and best count; else if no improvement for 100: lr=max(0.01, lr*0.95). But current_C based on hard.
   Stall: if no best improvement for stall_limit (e.g., 3000): reset a = best * exp(random perturb*0.05) / sum, reset optimizer, maybe change power? The outer power handles.

    a = normalize_positive(a0)
    n=len(a); N=2*n-1; L=next_pow2(N)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_m=conv_full_max(a)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # for each order in orders:
    for order in orders:
       if time > deadline break
       m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); iter_count=0; stall=0
       # maybe adapt lr per order: lr_order = lr * scale? For large order gradient sharper but normalized; same.

       while time.monotonic() < deadline:

Function:
```
def lbgf_fgrad(theta, n, L, k, scale):
   th=theta-mean
   a=softmax(th)
   p=conv
   z=k*p
   m=z.max(); w=exp(z-m); w/=w.sum()
   F=scale*(m + log(w.sum()))  # since w sum before norm? Actually after norm, sum_orig = exp(m)*sum; log = m+log(sum_exp). We can compute sum_exp before normalize.
   h=2corr(w,a)
   grad_a=scale*h
   grad_th=grad_a - a*np.dot(a,grad_a)
   return F, grad_th
```

def pair_refine(a0, deadline, seed=1, max_pairs=20, frac_list=None, verbose=False):
  a=norm
  p=conv; C=p.max(); best...
  t0=...
  rng=...
  stall=0
  if frac_list None: set global? Can't nested; define top-level constant list.
  while time<deadline:
     # active top
     top_elems = min(120, N)
     idx = np.argpartition(p, -top_elems)[-top_elems:]  # faster than sort
     active = idx
     # active p values sorted? maybe.
     # weights uniform active
     w = np.zeros(N); w[active]=1/len(active)
     h = gradient from w (FFT)
     # ensure h finite
     h_centered = h - np.dot(a,h)
     # top source: argpartition h_centered top 10 where a>1e-12
     src_idx = np.argpartition(h_centered, -10)[-10:]
     dst_idx = np.argpartition(h_centered, 10)[:10]
     # maybe include low a positions: low_a = argsort(a)[:10]; add to dst
     improved=False
     for s in src_idx:
       for d in dst_low combined:
          if s==d: continue
          # evaluate direction s->d and d->s
          best_delta=0; best_est=C
          for direction (src,dst) in [(s,d),(d,s)]:
             alpha=a[src]; if alpha<1e-12 continue
             # build lin? expensive per direction; can cache? We'll do
             lin = np.zeros(N) # memory per pair; too many zeros costly.
```
Creating lin array length N for each pair expensive but okay? N=8000, pairs 20 -> allocate 8000 floats *20 = small but 1GB ops. if k==2j: quad_k +=1

```

Define top-level:
```
def refine_cycle(a, deadline, orders, fracs, lr, seed=123):
    a_cur = normalize_positive(a)
    total = deadline - now()
    if total <=0 return a_cur
    # scale fracs sum
    ssum=sum(fracs)
    for order, frac in zip(orders, fracs):
        t = max(5, total * frac / ssum)
        new_a, new_m = adam_refine(a_cur, now()+t, [order], lr=lr, seed=seed)
        a_cur = normalize_positive(new_a)
        if time>=deadline break
    return a_cur
```
This top-level. No closures.

But `adam_refine` returns best_a (maybe from its internal best), not necessarily current. If run from a_cur and gets no improvement, returns best internal = a_cur normalized. Good.

Need ensure `adam_refine` doesn't spend too much on first order if order list length 1 and deadline. OK.

Thus L-BFGS fprime arguments: n,L,temp,scale. It returns F and gradient. Good.
```
def lbgf_fprime(theta,n,L,temp,scale):
   ...
   z = p / temp
   m = z.max()
   ez = np.exp(z-m)
   s = ez.sum()
   F = scale*(m + np.log(s))
   w = ez/s
   h = 2*corr(w,a)
   grad_a = scale*h/temp  # derivative of m+logs? Yes ∂F=scale*(1/temp)h.
   gtheta = grad_a - a*dot(a,grad_a); gtheta -= mean
   return F, gtheta
```
Here if temp = pmax0/focus, h/temp scale ~focus/pmax * 2/n; for n=4200, focus100 => ~0.48. Good.
If pmax decreases, z max decreases, F decreases; good. If sequence rotates with same max, optimal.
This objective is smooth and consistent with fixed temp; pmax dynamic only in z values. Good.

def adam_refine(a0, deadline, orders, lr=0.08, b1=0.9, b2=0.999, max_step=0.8, stall_limit=5000, seed=1, verbose=False):
    a = normalize_positive(np.asarray(a0, float))
    n = len(a)
    if n ==0: return a, inf
    N = 2*n-1; L=next_pow2(N)
    p = conv_full(a,L)
    best_m = float(p.max())
    best_a = a.copy()
    t0 = get_time()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    total = max(0.0, deadline - t0)
    if total <=0: return best_a,best_m
    # split time among orders: maybe more time for later? equal.
    if len(orders)==0: return
    # we can run orders sequentially with equal time, but if first order improves best, later start from current best? We'll start from current a? maybe after each order reset to best.

       if get_time() >= deadline: break
       # allocate equal remaining? If we call with multiple orders, equal share of total may leave no time. Better sequential until deadline, first order uses remaining? That's bad. Compute t_order = max(1.0, total / len(orders)) but not exceed deadline.

Make weights:
```
def make_weights_power(p, power):
    p = np.asarray(p, float)
    pmax = p.max()
    N = len(p)
    if pmax <= 0: return np.ones(N)/N
    rel = p / pmax
    # floors
    rel = np.where(rel > 0.0, rel, 0.0)
    # If power == 2: w = p / p.sum() (faster, exact)
    if power == 2:
       s=p.sum(); if s>0: return p/s
    log_rel = np.log(rel, where=rel>0, out=np.full_like(rel, -np.inf))? Simpler:
    rel_safe = np.maximum(rel, 1e-300)
    z = (power - 1) * np.log(rel_safe)
    z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
    w = np.exp(z)
    s = w.sum()
    if s <= 0:
       w = np.zeros_like(p); idx = np.argmax(p); w[idx]=1; return w
    return w/s
```
For large power, rel_safe floor gives nonzero weights to zeros after clip; but clip at -700 makes them exp(-700) ~1e-304, irrelevant. Good.
For power=2, p sum may not exactly 1 after max; OK.

while time<deadline:
   t+=1
   pmax = p.max()
   w=make_weights_power(p,order)
   W=rfft(w)
   h=2*irfft(W*conj(A))
   c = float(np.dot(a,h))
   dev = h - c
   scale = float(np.max(np.abs(dev)))
   if scale > 1e-15:
      q = dev/scale
      q = np.clip(q, -4.0, 4.0)
   else: q=zeros
   # numerical bias maybe
   b1t = 1 - (1-b1)**t
   b2t = 1 - (1-b2)**t
   m = b1*m + (1-b1)*q
   v = b2*v + (1-b2)*(q*q)
   mh = m / b1t
   vh = v / b2t
   step = current_lr * mh / (np.sqrt(vh) + 1e-8)
   step = np.clip(step, -max_step, max_step)
   loga = loga - step
   maxl = loga.max()
   loga = loga - maxl
   loga = np.maximum(loga, -700)
   a_new = np.exp(loga)
   s = a_new.sum()
   if not finite or s<=0:
       # revert
       current_lr *= 0.8; continue
   a_new = a_new / s
   loga = loga - np.log(s)

Now, `lbgf_fprime`: Need p, h. For efficiency, compute A, p; then z, w, W,h. That's 3 transforms. OK.
```
def lbgf_fprime(theta, n, L, temp, scale):
    LBFGS_STATE['count'] += 1
    if get_time() > LBFGS_DEADLINE or LBFGS_STATE['count']>LBFGS_MAX_EVAL: raise SearchTimeout
    LBFGS_STATE['x'] = np.asarray(theta, float).copy()
    th = theta - np.mean(theta)
    th = np.clip(th, -50.0, 50.0)
    a = np.exp(th - th.max()); s=a.sum(); if s<=0 return inf, zeros
    a /= s
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,L); p=np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)[:2*n-1]; p=np.maximum(p,0)
    z = p / temp
    m = z.max()
    ez = np.exp(z - m)
    se = ez.sum()
    if se <=0: F=scale*m; w=np.zeros...; 
    else: w=ez/se; F=scale*(m + np.log(se))
    W=np.fft.rfft(w,L); h=2*np.fft.irfft(W*np.conj(A),L)[:n]
    grad_a = scale * (h / temp)
    gth = grad_a - a * float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    gth -= float(np.mean(gth))
    # optionally scale down if large
    maxg = np.max(np.abs(gth)); if maxg > 10: gth *= 10/maxg? But scaling mismatch. Maybe not.
    return F, gth
```

For n4200 pmax1.8e-4/focus150=1.2e-6. h/temp ~ (1/n)/(5e-3/n)=200. Yes gradient ~200. F changes? F ~ logsumexp(p/temp), z~150, F~156. Small changes in pmax 1e-8 / temp =0.008. ```
def pair_refine(a0, deadline, seed=2, active_count=120, max_pairs=30, stall_limit=60, verbose=False):
    a=normalize; n;N,L; p=conv; C=p.max(); best...; t0
    rng...
    stall=0
    active_count=min(active_count, N)
    fracs=FRACS (global)
    while time<deadline:
        # active
        if len(p) <= active_count: active=np.arange(N)
        else: active=np.argpartition(p, N-active_count)[N-active_count:]
        # maybe include index of current max always
        imax=int(np.argmax(p)); if not in active, replace last.
        kact=active.astype(np.int64); pact=p[kact]
        # weights uniform active for h
        w=np.zeros(N); w[active]=1.0/len(active)
        A=rfft(a,L); W=rfft(w,L); h=2*irfft(W*np.conj(A),L)[:n]
        hc = h - np.dot(a,h)
        # candidates
        src_list = unique(concatenate(argpartition(hc,-12)[-12:], argpartition(a, -12)[-12:]))
        dst_list = unique(concatenate(argpartition(hc,12)[:12], argpartition(a,12)[:12]))
        # random few if no
        pairs=[]
        for s in src_list:
          for d in dst_list:
            if s!=d: pairs.append((s,d))
            if len(pairs)>=max_pairs: break
        if not pairs: random...
        improved=False
        for (s,d) in pairs:
           if C >= best_m*(1-1e-15) and no? evaluate both directions
           for src,dst in ((s,d),(d,s)):
              alpha = a[src]
              if alpha <= 1e-14: continue
              beta = a[dst]
              # lin/quad
              lin = np.zeros_like(pact)
              # mask source
              m1 = (kact >= src) & (kact < src+n)
              if np.any(m1): lin[m1] += -2.0 * a[kact[m1]-src]
              m2 = (kact >= dst) & (kact < dst+n)
              if np.any(m2): lin[m2] += 2.0 * a[kact[m2]-dst]
              cross = (kact == src + dst)
              if np.any(cross): lin[cross] += -4.0 * (alpha - beta)
              quad = np.zeros_like(pact)
              d1 = 2*src
              m = (kact == d1); quad[m] += 1.0
              d2 = 2*dst; m=(kact==d2); quad[m]+=1.0
              if np.any(cross): quad[cross] += -2.0
              # evaluate deltas
              best_delta = 0.0; best_est = float(np.max(pact))
              for frac in fracs:
                 delta = alpha * frac
                 if delta <=0: continue
                 vals = pact + delta*lin + delta*delta*quad
                 est = float(vals.max())
                 if est < best_est:
                     best_est=est; best_delta=delta
              if best_delta > 0 and best_est < C * 0.9995:
                  a_trial = a.copy()
                  a_trial[src] = alpha - best_delta
                  a_trial[dst] = beta + best_delta
                  a_trial = np.maximum(a_trial,0.0)
                  ssum = a_trial.sum()
                  if ssum < 0.01: continue
                  a_trial /= ssum
                  p_trial = conv_full(a_trial, L)
                  p_trial = np.maximum(p_trial,0)
                  C_trial = float(p_trial.max())
                  if C_trial < C - 1e-16 and C_trial < best_m or C_trial < best_m - 1e-18? Actually accept if improves current; update best if best_m.
                      a=a_trial; p=p_trial; C=C_trial
                      if C < best_m: best_m=C; best_a=a.copy()
                      improved=True; stall=0
                      # after accept, break pair/direction maybe recompute active? We can break out to outer to refresh.
          if improved: break
        if improved: continue
        # no improvement: stall, random moves
        stall+=1
        if stall > 1:
           # try 10 random pairs with random delta
           for _ in range(20): ...
        if stall > stall_limit:
           # perturb from best
           loga=np.log(best_a+eps)+rng.normal(0,0.01)
           a=log_to_a(loga); p=conv; C=...
           m? no momentum
           if C<best_m update
           stall=0
        if time>deadline break
    return best_a,best_m
```

`get_time`:
```
def get_time():
    return time.monotonic()
```

`next_pow2`:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    m=int(m)
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```

`normalize_positive`:
```
def normalize_positive(a):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim !=1:
       a=a.reshape(-1)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    n=a.size
    if n ==0:
       return np.ones(1)
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
       return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)/n
    return a/s
```

`log_to_a`:
```
def log_to_a(loga):
    loga=np.asarray(loga, float)
    if loga.size ==0: return np.ones(1)
    loga=np.where(np.isfinite(loga), loga, -700.0)
    loga=loga - float(loga.max())
    loga=np.maximum(loga, -700.0)
    a=np.exp(loga)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if not finite or s<=0: return np.ones_like(a)/len(a)
    return a/s
```

`conv_full`:
```
def conv_full(a, L=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    N=2*n-1
    if L is None: L=next_pow2(N)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    p=np.fft.irfft(A*A, L)[:N]
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    return p
```
`conv_full_max`: `return float(np.max(conv_full(a,L)))` if N>0.

`objective_score`:
```
def objective_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    if a.size==0: return inf
    a=normalize_positive(a)
    m=conv_full_max(a)
    return float(2.0*a.size*m)
```
Note if a not normalized, normalize changes score invariant. Good.

`make_weights_power` as above.

`gradient_h`:
```
def gradient_h(a,w,L):
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,L); W=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
    h=np.fft.irfft(W*np.conj(A), L)[:a.size]
    return 2.0*h
```

`adam_refine` single order:
Need use global time. Let's code:
```
def adam_refine(a0, deadline, order, lr=0.08, b1=0.9, b2=0.999, max_step=0.8, stall_limit=4000, seed=1, verbose=False):
    a=normalize_positive(a0)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return a, float('inf')
    N=2*n-1; L=next_pow2(N)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    p=np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)[:N]
    p=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0.0)
    C=float(np.max(p)) if p.size else 0.0
    best_a=a.copy(); best_m=C
    t0=get_time()
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    if order is None or order < 2:
        return best_a,best_m
    loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n)
    it=0; stall=0; cur_lr=lr
    restart_count=0
    while get_time() < deadline:
        it += 1
        pmax = float(p.max())
        if not np.isfinite(pmax) or pmax <= 1e-300:
            break
        w = make_weights_power(p, order)
        W = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
        h = 2.0 * np.fft.irfft(W * np.conj(A), L)[:n]
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
            # fallback reset
            h = np.zeros(n)
        c = float(np.dot(a, h))
        if not np.isfinite(c): c=0.0
        dev = h - c
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(dev)))
        if scale > 1e-15:
            q = dev / scale
            q = np.clip(q, -4.0, 4.0)
        else:
            q = np.zeros(n)
        b1hat = 1.0 - (1.0 - b1) ** it
        b2hat = 1.0 - (1.0 - b2) ** it
        m = b1*m + (1.0-b1)*q
        v = b2*v + (1.0-b2)*(q*q)
        mh = m / b1hat
        vh = v / b2hat
        step = cur_lr * mh / (np.sqrt(vh) + 1e-8)
        step = np.clip(step, -max_step, max_step)
        loga = loga - step
        maxl = float(loga.max())
        loga = loga - maxl
        loga = np.maximum(loga, -700.0)
        a_new = np.exp(loga)
        s_new = float(a_new.sum())
        if (not np.isfinite(s_new)) or s_new <= 0.0:
            cur_lr = max(0.005, cur_lr * 0.5)
            # recompute? a/p unchanged
            continue
        a_new = a_new / s_new
        loga = loga - float(np.log(s_new))
        A_new = np.fft.rfft(a_new, L)
        p_new = np.fft.irfft(A_new * A_new, L)[:N]
        p_new = np.where(np.isfinite(p_new), p_new, 0.0)
        C_new = float(np.max(p_new))
        accepted = True
        if (not np.isfinite(C_new)) or C_new > C * 2.0 or C_new > best_m * 2.0:
            accepted = False
        if accepted:
            a = a_new; p = p_new; A = A_new; C = C_new
            if C_new < C_prev? We lost C_prev; use if C_new < C? false. Need store C_old before update.
```
Need store C_old = C before update for lr adapt. Let's add before:

Now, `lbgf_fprime` details with global state. Top-level:
```
def lbgf_fprime(theta, n, L, temp, scale):
    LBFGS_STATE['count'] = LBFGS_STATE['count'] + 1
    if get_time() > LBFGS_DEADLINE or LBFGS_STATE['count'] > LBFGS_MAX_EVAL:
        raise SearchTimeout
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    LBFGS_STATE['x'] = theta.copy()
    th = theta - float(np.mean(theta))
    th = np.clip(th, -50.0, 50.0)
    a = np.exp(th - float(np.max(th)))
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
        z = np.zeros(n)
        return 1e30, z
    a = a / s
    N=2*n-1
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    p=np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)[:N]
    p=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0.0)
    zval = p / temp
    zmax = float(np.max(zval))
    ez = np.exp(zval - zmax)
    se = float(np.sum(ez))
    if se <= 0 or not finite:
        w = np.zeros(N); if N>0: w[int(np.argmax(p))]=1
        F = scale * zmax
    else:
        w = ez / se
        F = scale * (zmax + np.log(se))
    W=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
    h=2.0*np.fft.irfft(W*np.conj(A), L)[:n]
    grad_a = scale * (h / temp)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_a)): grad_a=np.zeros(n)
    gth = grad_a - a * float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    gth = gth - float(np.mean(gth))
    # Clip for L-BFGS?
    gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(gth)))
    if gmax > 100.0:
       gth *= 100.0/gmax
    return float(F), gth
```
Scaling gradient without scaling objective mismatch but just clip; if gmax huge due temp small. Maybe skip clipping to preserve. But L-BFGS may fail if norm huge. `scale` chosen to moderate. For bad initial, pmax maybe high, temp=pmax0/focus fixed, grad ~focus. For n=4000, focus300 grad~300? scale=1/focus -> grad~1? Wait scale=1/focus, yes. If grad_a = scale*h/temp; h/temp~2*focus/(n pmax?) ~ O(focus? actually h~1/n, temp=pmax/focus ~0.75/(nfocus) => h/temp ~ (1/n)/(0.75/(nfocus))=1.33*focus. scale=1/focus ->1.33. Good. Clip 100 not needed.
F scale =1/focus; F~1. Fine.

def lbgf_cb(step, fval):
    if get_time() > LBFGS_DEADLINE or LBFGS_STATE['count'] > LBFGS_MAX_EVAL:
        raise SearchTimeout
```

`lbgf_polish`:
```
def lbgf_polish(a0, deadline, focus=100, max_iter=300, max_eval=30000, verbose=False):
    global LBFGS_DEADLINE, LBFGS_MAX_EVAL
    a=normalize_positive(a0)
    n=a.size
    if n==0 or get_time() >= deadline: return a, conv_max(a)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_m=conv_max(a)
    if not SCIPY_OPT_AVAILABLE:
       # fallback small adam? no
       return best_a,best_m
    N=2*n-1; L=next_pow2(N)
    p0=conv_full(a,L); pmax0=float(p0.max())
    if pmax0 <= 0: return
    temp = max(pmax0 / max(1.0, float(focus)), 1e-12)
    scale = 1.0 / max(1.0, float(focus))
    theta = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
    theta = theta - float(np.mean(theta))
    theta = np.clip(theta, -50.0, 50.0)
    LBFGS_STATE['x']=theta.copy(); LBFGS_STATE['count']=0
    LBFGS_DEADLINE=deadline; LBFGS_MAX_EVAL=max_eval
    try:
        res = fmin_l_bfgs_b(lbgf_fprime, theta, args=(n,L,temp,scale), maxiter=max_iter, ftol=1e-12, pgtol=1e-8, maxcor=20, maxls=20, callback=lbgf_cb, disp=0)
        theta_out = np.asarray(res[0], float)
    except Exception:
        theta_out = LBFGS_STATE['x'] if LBFGS_STATE['x'] is not None else theta
    a2 = log_to_a(theta_out)
    m2=conv_full_max(a2,L)
    if m2 < best_m: best_m=m2; best_a=a2
    # maybe also evaluate exactly normalized? log_to_a normalized.
    return best_a, best_m
```

Potential: `fmin_l_bfgs_b` may not accept `callback` raising because it expects no? It does. If exception not SearchTimeout due memory, catch and use state.

`pair_refine` as detailed. Need `unique_concat` helper top-level:
```
def unique_indices(arr, max_count):
    arr=np.asarray(arr).reshape(-1)
    if arr.size ==0: return np.array([], dtype=int)
    arr=np.unique(arr)
    if arr.size > max_count: arr=arr[:max_count]
    return arr.astype(int)
```

```
if stall > 1:
   # random pair move 10 times
   for _ in range(10):
      i=int(rng.integers(n)); j=int(rng.integers(n)); if i!=j and a[i]>1e-12:
         delta=a[i]*float(rng.uniform(0.01,0.2))
         a_trial=a.copy(); a_trial[i]-=delta; a_trial[j]+=delta; normalize; p_trial; C_trial; if C_trial<C accept improved=True
   if improved: stall=0; continue
stall +=1
if stall > stall_limit:
   # reset from best with noise
   loga=np.log(np.maximum(best_a,1e-300)) + rng.normal(0, 0.01, n)
   a=log_to_a(loga); p=conv...; C=...
   if C<best_m update
   stall=0
```

`interpolate_sequence`:
```
def interpolate_sequence(a,new_n):
   a=normalize_positive(a); old_n=a.size
   if old_n == new_n: return a.copy()
   if old_n == 1: return np.full(new_n, a[0])
   xold=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,old_n)
   xnew=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,new_n)
   val=np.interp(xnew,xold,a)
   return normalize_positive(val)
```
Could endpoint values overweight? Since density; when scaling to sum1, endpoint interpolation linear in index. Fine.

def make_power_ramp(n, power=0.5):
   x=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
   a=x**power
   return normalize_positive(a)

Let's write `pair_refine` carefully. It uses `FRACS` global.
Pseudo code fully:
```
PAIR_FRACS = np.array([0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.15,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.7,0.9])

def pair_refine(a0, deadline, seed=2, active_count=120, topcand=12, max_pairs=40, stall_limit=70, verbose=False):
    a = normalize_positive(a0)
    n=a.size; N=2*n-1; L=next_pow2(N)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,L); p=...; C=...
    best_a=a.copy(); best_m=C
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    stall=0; it=0
    if deadline <= get_time(): return best_a,best_m
    while get_time() < deadline:
       it +=1
       if C <= 0: break
       # active
       top_active = min(active_count, N)
       if top_active < N:
          active = np.argpartition(p, N-top_active)[N-top_active:]
       else: active=np.arange(N)
       imax=int(np.argmax(p))
       if not np.any(active == imax): active[-1]=imax
       kact=active.astype(np.int64)
       pact=p[kact].astype(np.float64)
       # h gradient active
       w=np.zeros(N); w[active]=1.0/len(active)
       W=np.fft.rfft(w,L); h=2*np.fft.irfft(W*np.conj(A),L)[:n]
       hc = h - float(np.dot(a,h))
       if not finite hc: hc=zeros
       # candidate arrays
       idx_h_top = np.argpartition(hc, -topcand)[-topcand:] if n>topcand else np.arange(n)
       idx_a_top = np.argpartition(a, -topcand)[-topcand:] if n>topcand else np.arange(n)
       idx_h_bot = np.argpartition(hc, topcand)[:topcand] if n>topcand else np.arange(n)
       idx_a_bot = np.argpartition(a, topcand)[:topcand] if n>topcand else np.arange(n)
       src = unique_indices(np.concatenate((idx_h_top, idx_a_top)), topcand*2)
       dst = unique_indices(np.concatenate((idx_h_bot, idx_a_bot)), topcand*2)
       pairs_list=[]
       for s in src:
          for d in dst:
             if s != d:
                pairs_list.append((int(s), int(d)))
             if len(pairs_list) >= max_pairs: break
          if len(pairs_list)>=max_pairs: break
       if not pairs_list:
          i=int(rng.integers(n)); j=int(rng.integers(n)); pairs_list=[(i,j) if i!=j else (0,1)]
       improved=False
       for s,d in pairs_list:
          if improved: break
          for src_i, dst_j in ((s,d),(d,s)):
             alpha=float(a[src_i]); beta=float(a[dst_j])
             if alpha <= 1e-14: continue
             lin=np.zeros_like(pact)
             m1=(kact >= src_i) & (kact < src_i + n)
             if np.any(m1): lin[m1] += -2.0 * a[kact[m1]-src_i]
             m2=(kact >= dst_j) & (kact < dst_j + n)
             if np.any(m2): lin[m2] += 2.0 * a[kact[m2]-dst_j]
             cross = (kact == src_i + dst_j)
             if np.any(cross): lin[cross] += -4.0 * (alpha - beta)
             quad=np.zeros_like(pact)
             mdiag=(kact == (2*src_i)); if np.any: quad[mdiag]+=1
             mdiag=(kact == (2*dst_j)); if np.any: quad[mdiag]+=1
             if np.any(cross): quad[cross] += -2.0
             best_delta=0.0; best_est=float(np.max(pact))
             for frac in PAIR_FRACS:
                delta=alpha*float(frac)
                if delta <=0: continue
                vals = pact + delta*lin + delta*delta*quad
                est=float(np.max(vals))
                if est < best_est:
                   best_est=est; best_delta=delta
             if best_delta > 0 and best_est < C*(1.0 - 1e-9):
                at=a.copy()
                at[src_i]=alpha-best_delta
                at[dst_j]=beta+best_delta
                at=np.maximum(at,0.0); ss=at.sum(); if ss<1e-12: continue
                at/=ss
                pt=conv_full(at,L); pt=np.maximum(pt,0); Ct=float(pt.max())
                if Ct < C - 1e-14:
                    a=at; p=pt; A=np.fft.rfft(a,L); C=Ct
                    if C < best_m:
                       best_m=C; best_a=a.copy()
                    improved=True
                    break
       if improved:
          stall=0
          continue
       # no improvement random
       rand_improved=False
       for _ in range(10):
          i=int(rng.integers(n)); j=int(rng.integers(n))
          if i==j or a[i] <=1e-14: continue
          delta=float(a[i])*float(rng.uniform(0.005,0.1))
          at=a.copy(); at[i]=a[i]-delta; at[j]=a[j]+delta; at=np.maximum(at,0); ss=at.sum(); if ss<1e-12: continue; at/=ss
          pt=conv_full(at,L); Ct=float(pt.max())
          if Ct < C - 1e-14:
             a=at; p=pt; A=...; C=Ct; if C<best_m update; rand_improved=True; break
       if rand_improved:
          stall=0; continue
       stall +=1
       if stall > stall_limit:
          loga=np.log(np.maximum(best_a,1e-300)) + rng.normal(0,0.01,n)
          a=log_to_a(loga)
          A=...; p=...; C=...
          if C<best_m update
          stall=0
    return best_a,best_m
```
After reset from best, C should =best_m. Good.
Potential bug: In dir move, `at[src_i]=alpha-best_delta` but if src_i==dst_j? We check s!=d; directions from pair s,d, so src_i != dst_j. Good. If alpha<delta? deltas fractions <=0.9*alpha, ok.
The `pact` active values do not include updates from previous pair in same outer (since no improved). OK.
`C*(1 -1e-9)` for C~1e-4, threshold 1e-13; small. Good.

`unique_indices` top-level:
```
def unique_indices(arr, max_count):
   arr=np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.int64).reshape(-1)
   if arr.size ==0: return np.array([], dtype=np.int64)
   arr=np.unique(arr)
   if arr.size > max_count: arr=arr[:max_count]
   return arr
```

Implement in make_weights_power:
```
if power == 2: w=p/p.sum()
elif power == 4:
   rel=p/pmax; w=p*rel*rel; s=w.sum(); return w/s if s>0
elif power == 16:
   rel=p/pmax; w=p*rel**15? direct may underflow? p ~1e-4, rel^15 ~1e-45, underflow? p*rel^15 ~1e-49, still above 1e-308? 1e-49 okay. For order=64, rel^63 ~1e-30 for non-max, p~1e-34, underflow? Underflow to 0; okay for max? max rel=1. We can direct for power<=32 using `np.power(rel, power-1)`.
single = rel ** (power-1)
```

def make_weights_power(p, power):
   pmax=p.max(); if pmax<=0: uniform
   if power == 2:
       w = p; s=w.sum(); if s>0 return w/s else max
   if power <= 64:
       rel = p / pmax
       w = np.power(rel, power - 1)
       s=w.sum()
       if s>0 and finite: return w/s
   # large
   lower = pmax * (1.0 - 1.0 / float(power))
   above = p >= lower
   w = np.zeros_like(p)
   if np.any(above):
       w[above] = (p[above] - lower)
   s=w.sum()
   if s <=0:
       idx=np.argmax(p); w[idx]=1; return w
   return w/s
```

Product X P Z: ifft(Z)[r]=1/L∑ ∑_n x[n] e^{-2πi nk/L} ∑_m y[m] e^{-2πi mk/L} e^{2πi rk/L} = ∑_{n,m} x_n y_m 1/L∑ e^{-2πi (n+m-r) k/L} = ∑_{n+m = r} x_n y_m. Ah! I made sign error before: A*A product exponent e^{-i (n+m)r}. So c_r=∑_{n+m=r} a_n a_m. That's convolution! Good. For A*A with x=a,y=a: r=0: a0a0 = a0²; r=1: a0a1+a1a0=2a0a1; r=2: a1². Correct. Great.
For correlation product W * conj(A): W[k]=∑ w_m e^{-i mk}; conj(A)[k]=∑ a_n e^{+i nk}; product sum exponent e^{i(n-m)k}. ifft: 1/L∑ e^{i(n-m)k} e^{i rk}=δ_{n-m+r?} = if n-m+r ≡0 => r=m-n. Result c_r=∑_{m-n=r} w_m a_n = ∑_n a_n w_{n+r}. Desired with r=j: ∑_n a_n w_{n+j}. Good! So both formulas now correct. Phew.
Thus conv and gradient correct.

Now verify pair move formula matches convolution p_k =∑_{i+j=k} a_i a_j. For i,j distinct, term p_{i+j} includes a_i a_j and a_j a_i separately if i≠j? In convolution sum over all ordered pairs (i,l) with i+l=k. If k=i+j and i≠j, terms (i,j) and (j,i) both included, total 2a_i a_j. Our formulas use factor 2 for off-diagonal. Good.

Now, check gradient h_j =2∑_k w_k a_{k-j}. For fixed j, valid k such that 0≤k-j≤n-1. In convolution p_k includes a_j a_{k-j}; derivative 2 a_{k-j} (including k=2j diagonal). Good.

Everything consistent.

Now, final code. Need be careful with `np.argpartition` when N-top_active negative? top_active <=N. If top_active == N, use arange.

Potential issue in pair_refine: `if np.any(m1): lin[m1] += -2.0 * a[kact[m1] - src_i]` Here `lin[m1]` and `a[...]` arrays same length; works. If `lin` is float64.
`mdiag=(kact == (2*src_i))`, if 2*src_i maybe int.

Now, `unique_indices` if max_count less than arr size. Good.

Let's consider if `a` length very small (< topcand), argpartition df. In pair_refine, n likely large; but robust:
```
if n > topcand: idx=...
else: idx=np.arange(n)
```
OK.

Potential issue: `np.dot(a,h)` for a,h length n. Good.

Now, direct `objective_score` recomputes conv for local score often. For initial best from parent, okay.

Let's ensure all functions defined before use. Order: imports, constants, exceptions, globals, helpers, propose.

Let's write final code now. Ensure docstring at top.

One more thought: Because p max could be at endpoint if a0 large, but our algorithms don't assume central. Weights active include endpoints. Good.

Potential under/overflow in `np.exp(loga)` with loga max subtract and floor -700; exp(-700)=9e-305, sum not zero. Good. If n small and many -700, sum maybe from max 1. Good.

Adam: `b1hat = 1.0 - (1.0 - b1) ** it`. For it=0? It starts 1 after increment. Good. If it large, pow 0, b1hat 1. Good.
`v` maybe zero for initial, step zero? q maybe nonzero, v after update, b2hat. OK.

In `adam_refine`, if order threshold high, weights sum maybe exactly count; fine.

Potential performance: `np.all(np.isfinite(h))` each iter O(n). Could remove or check max. Use `if not np.isfinite(np.max(h))` maybe. But `np.all` okay. Could add overhead. Optimize: skip check unless trouble. Remove to save. But if NaN, step NaN. We can after h: `if not np.isfinite(h).all():` O(n) small relative FFT. Keep.

In `lbgf_fprime`, clipping gradient to max 1000 maybe. Use if gmax > 1000. Good.

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative sequence a minimizing
    2 * n * max(convolve(a, a, mode='full')) / (sum(a))**2.

The implementation uses:
1. FFT-based evaluation of full convolution and gradients.
2. Smoothed L_r / log-sum-exp approximations to the max coefficient, with
   Adam in the log-domain on the probability simplex.
3. A small quasi-Newton polish using scipy L-BFGS-B when available.
4. Exact local pair-mass transfer refinement targeting active convolution peaks.
5. Multi-length interpolation and final polishing.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import fmin_l_bfgs_b
    SCIPY_OPT_AVAILABLE = True
except Exception:
    SCIPY_OPT_AVAILABLE = False


class SearchTimeout(Exception):
    pass


PAIR_FRACS = np.array([0.005, 0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.15, 0.20,
                       0.30, 0.50, 0.70, 0.90], dtype=np.float64)

LBFGS_DEADLINE = 0.0
LBFGS_MAX_EVAL = 100000
LBFGS_STATE = {"x": None, "count": 0}


def get_time():
    return time.monotonic()


def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_positive(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.reshape(-1)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return a / s


def log_to_a(loga):
    loga = np.asarray(loga, dtype=np.float64)
    if loga.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    loga = np.where(np.isfinite(loga), loga, -700.0)
    loga = loga - float(loga.max())
    loga = np.maximum(loga, -700.0)
    a = np.exp(loga)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones_like(a, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def conv_full(a, L=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    N = 2 * n - 1
    if L is None:
        L = next_pow2(N)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    p = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)[:N]
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    return p


def conv_full_max(a, L=None):
    p = conv_full(a, L)
    if p.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(np.max(p))


def local_score(a):
    a = normalize_positive(a)
    m = conv_full_max(a)
    sc = 2.0 * float(a.size) * m
    return a, m, float(sc)


def make_weights_power(p, power):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    N = p.size
    if N == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    pmax = float(np.max(p))
    if not np.isfinite(pmax) or pmax <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64) / float(N)

    if power <= 1:
        return np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64) / float(N)

    if power == 2:
        s = float(np.sum(p))
        if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
            return p / s
        w = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(p))] = 1.0
        return w

    if power <= 64:
        rel = p / pmax
        with np.errstate(under="ignore", over="ignore", invalid="ignore", divide="ignore"):
            w = np.power(rel, int(power) - 1)
        w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
        s = float(np.sum(w))
        if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
            return w / s

    # For larger powers, use a cheap sharp thresholdized weighting.
    lower = pmax * (1.0 - 1.0 / float(power))
    w = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
    above = p >= lower
    if np.any(above):
        w[above] = p[above] - lower
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(p))
        w[idx] = 1.0
        return w
    return w / s


def gradient_h(a, w, L):
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    h = np.fft.irfft(W * np.conj(A), L)[:a.size]
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
    return 2.0 * h


def adam_refine(a0, deadline, order, lr=0.08, b1=0.9, b2=0.999,
                max_step=0.8, stall_limit=8000, seed=1, verbose=False):
    a = normalize_positive(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a, float("inf")

    N = 2 * n - 1
    L = next_pow2(N)

    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    p = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)[:N]
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    C = float(np.max(p)) if p.size else 0.0

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_m = C

    t0 = get_time()
    if get_time() >= deadline or order is None or order < 2:
        return best_a, best_m

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    it = 0
    stall = 0
    cur_lr = float(lr)

    while get_time() < deadline:
        it += 1

        pmax = float(np.max(p))
        if not np.isfinite(pmax) or pmax <= 1e-300:
            break

        w = make_weights_power(p, order)
        h = gradient_h(a, w, L)

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
            h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

        c = float(np.dot(a, h))
        if not np.isfinite(c):
            c = 0.0

        dev = h - c
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(dev)))
        if scale > 1e-15:
            q = dev / scale
            q = np.clip(q, -4.0, 4.0)
            q = np.where(np.isfinite(q), q, 0.0)
        else:
            q = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

        b1hat = 1.0 - (1.0 - b1) ** it
        b2hat = 1.0 - (1.0 - b2) ** it

        m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * q
        v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * (q * q)

        mh = m / max(b1hat, 1e-16)
        vh = v / max(b2hat, 1e-16)

        step = cur_lr * mh / (np.sqrt(vh) + 1e-8)
        step = np.clip(step, -max_step, max_step)
        step = np.where(np.isfinite(step), step, 0.0)

        loga = loga - step
        maxl = float(np.max(loga))
        loga = loga - maxl
        loga = np.maximum(loga, -700.0)

        a_new = np.exp(loga)
        s_new = float(np.sum(a_new))
        if not np.isfinite(s_new) or s_new <= 0.0:
            cur_lr = max(0.002, cur_lr * 0.5)
            continue

        a_new = a_new / s_new
        loga = loga - float(np.log(s_new))

        A_new = np.fft.rfft(a_new, L)
        p_new = np.fft.irfft(A_new * A_new, L)[:N]
        p_new = np.where(np.isfinite(p_new), p_new, 0.0)
        p_new = np.maximum(p_new, 0.0)
        C_new = float(np.max(p_new))

        accepted = True
        if (not np.isfinite(C_new)) or C_new > C * 2.0 or C_new > best_m * 2.0:
            accepted = False

        C_old = C
        if accepted:
            a = a_new
            p = p_new
            A = A_new
            C = C_new

            if C_new < C_old:
                cur_lr = min(1.0, cur_lr * 1.01)
            elif C_new > C_old * 1.1:
                cur_lr = max(0.002, cur_lr * 0.90)

        if C_new < best_m - 1e-18:
            best_m = C_new
            best_a = a.copy()
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1

        if stall > stall_limit:
            noise_scale = 0.02 if order < 8 else (0.01 if order < 64 else 0.005)
            loga = np.log(np.maximum(best_a, 1e-300)) + rng.normal(0.0, noise_scale, n)
            a = log_to_a(loga)
            loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
            A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
            p = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)[:N]
            p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
            p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
            C = float(np.max(p))
            m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            it = 0
            stall = 0
            cur_lr = float(lr)

            if C < best_m:
                best_m = C
                best_a = a.copy()

        if verbose and (it % 5000 == 0):
            elapsed = get_time() - t0
            print(f"    adam order={order} it={it} t={elapsed:.0f}s "
                  f"score={2.0*n*best_m:.8f} stall={stall} lr={cur_lr:.4f}")

    return best_a, best_m


def unique_indices(arr, max_count):
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.int64).reshape(-1)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.int64)
    arr = np.unique(arr)
    if arr.size > max_count:
        arr = arr[:max_count]
    return arr


def pair_refine(a0, deadline, seed=2, active_count=120, topcand=12,
                max_pairs=40, stall_limit=80, verbose=False):
    a = normalize_positive(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a, float("inf")

    N = 2 * n - 1
    L = next_pow2(N)

    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    p = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)[:N]
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    C = float(np.max(p)) if p.size else 0.0

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_m = C

    if get_time() >= deadline or C <= 0.0:
        return best_a, best_m

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    stall = 0
    it = 0

    while get_time() < deadline:
        it += 1

        top_active = min(int(active_count), N)
        if top_active < N:
            active = np.argpartition(p, N - top_active)[N - top_active:]
        else:
            active = np.arange(N)

        imax = int(np.argmax(p))
        if not np.any(active == imax):
            active[-1] = imax

        kact = active.astype(np.int64, copy=False)
        pact = p[kact].astype(np.float64, copy=False)

        w = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
        w[active] = 1.0 / float(len(active))
        h = gradient_h(a, w, L)
        hc = h - float(np.dot(a, h))
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(hc)):
            hc = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

        if n > topcand:
            idx_h_top = np.argpartition(hc, -topcand)[-topcand:]
            idx_a_top = np.argpartition(a, -topcand)[-topcand:]
            idx_h_bot = np.argpartition(hc, topcand)[:topcand]
            idx_a_bot = np.argpartition(a, topcand)[:topcand]
        else:
            idx_h_top = np.arange(n)
            idx_a_top = np.arange(n)
            idx_h_bot = np.arange(n)
            idx_a_bot = np.arange(n)

        src = unique_indices(np.concatenate((idx_h_top, idx_a_top)), max(1, 2 * topcand))
        dst = unique_indices(np.concatenate((idx_h_bot, idx_a_bot)), max(1, 2 * topcand))

        pairs = []
        for s in src:
            for d in dst:
                if s != d:
                    pairs.append((int(s), int(d)))
                if len(pairs) >= max_pairs:
                    break
            if len(pairs) >= max_pairs:
                break

        if len(pairs) == 0:
            i = int(rng.integers(n))
            j = int(rng.integers(n))
            if i == j:
                j = (j + 1) % n
            pairs = [(i, j)]

        improved = False

        for s, d in pairs:
            if improved:
                break

            for src_i, dst_j in ((s, d), (d, s)):
                alpha = float(a[src_i])
                beta = float(a[dst_j])
                if alpha <= 1e-14:
                    continue

                lin = np.zeros_like(pact)

                m1 = (kact >= src_i) & (kact < src_i + n)
                if np.any(m1):
                    lin[m1] += -2.0 * a[kact[m1] - src_i]

                m2 = (kact >= dst_j) & (kact < dst_j + n)
                if np.any(m2):
                    lin[m2] += 2.0 * a[kact[m2] - dst_j]

                cross = (kact == (src_i + dst_j))
                if np.any(cross):
                    lin[cross] += -4.0 * (alpha - beta)

                quad = np.zeros_like(pact)
                md = (kact == (2 * src_i))
                if np.any(md):
                    quad[md] += 1.0

                md = (kact == (2 * dst_j))
                if np.any(md):
                    quad[md] += 1.0

                if np.any(cross):
                    quad[cross] += -2.0

                best_delta = 0.0
                best_est = float(np.max(pact))

                for frac in PAIR_FRACS:
                    delta = alpha * float(frac)
                    if delta <= 0.0:
                        continue
                    vals = pact + delta * lin + delta * delta * quad
                    est = float(np.max(vals))
                    if est < best_est:
                        best_est = est
                        best_delta = delta

                if best_delta > 0.0 and best_est < C * (1.0 - 1e-9):
                    at = a.copy()
                    at[src_i] = alpha - best_delta
                    at[dst_j] = beta + best_delta
                    at = np.maximum(at, 0.0)
                    ss = float(at.sum())
                    if not np.isfinite(ss) or ss <= 0.0:
                        continue
                    at /= ss

                    pt = conv_full(at, L)
                    Ct = float(np.max(pt))
                    if Ct < C - 1e-14:
                        a = at
                        p = pt
                        A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
                        C = Ct
                        if C < best_m:
                            best_m = C
                            best_a = a.copy()
                        improved = True
                        break

        if improved:
            stall = 0
            continue

        rand_improved = False
        for _ in range(10):
            i = int(rng.integers(n))
            j = int(rng.integers(n))
            if i == j or a[i] <= 1e-14:
                continue
            delta = float(a[i]) * float(rng.uniform(0.005, 0.10))
            at = a.copy()
            at[i] = a[i] - delta
            at[j] = a[j] + delta
            at = np.maximum(at, 0.0)
            ss = float(at.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(ss) or ss <= 0.0:
                continue
            at /= ss
            pt = conv_full(at, L)
            Ct = float(np.max(pt))
            if Ct < C - 1e-14:
                a = at
                p = pt
                A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
                C = Ct
                if C < best_m:
                    best_m = C
                    best_a = a.copy()
                rand_improved = True
                break

        if rand_improved:
            stall = 0
            continue

        stall += 1
        if stall > stall_limit:
            loga = np.log(np.maximum(best_a, 1e-300)) + rng.normal(0.0, 0.01, n)
            a = log_to_a(loga)
            A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
            p = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)[:N]
            p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
            p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
            C = float(np.max(p))
            if C < best_m:
                best_m = C
                best_a = a.copy()
            stall = 0

        if verbose and (it % 100 == 0):
            elapsed = get_time()
            print(f"    pair it={it} t={elapsed:.0f}s score={2.0*n*best_m:.8f} stall={stall}")

    return best_a, best_m


def lbgf_fprime(theta, n, L, temp, scale):
    LBFGS_STATE["count"] = LBFGS_STATE["count"] + 1
    if get_time() > LBFGS_DEADLINE or LBFGS_STATE["count"] > LBFGS_MAX_EVAL:
        raise SearchTimeout

    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    LBFGS_STATE["x"] = theta.copy()

    th = theta - float(np.mean(theta))
    th = np.clip(th, -50.0, 50.0)
    a = np.exp(th - float(np.max(th)))
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return 1e30, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / s

    N = 2 * n - 1
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    p = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)[:N]
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)

    z = p / temp
    zmax = float(np.max(z))
    ez = np.exp(z - zmax)
    se = float(np.sum(ez))

    if not np.isfinite(se) or se <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(p))] = 1.0
        F = scale * zmax
    else:
        w = ez / se
        F = scale * (zmax + float(np.log(se)))

    h = gradient_h(a, w, L)
    grad_a = scale * (h / temp)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_a)):
        grad_a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    gth = grad_a - a * float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    gth = gth - float(np.mean(gth))

    gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(gth)))
    if gmax > 1000.0:
        gth *= 1000.0 / gmax

    return float(F), gth.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def lbgf_callback(step, fval):
    if get_time() > LBFGS_DEADLINE or LBFGS_STATE["count"] > LBFGS_MAX_EVAL:
        raise SearchTimeout


def lbgf_polish(a0, deadline, focus=100.0, max_iter=250, max_eval=30000, verbose=False):
    global LBFGS_DEADLINE, LBFGS_MAX_EVAL

    a = normalize_positive(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a, float("inf")

    m0 = conv_full_max(a)
    if get_time() >= deadline or m0 <= 0.0:
        return a, m0

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_m = m0

    if not SCIPY_OPT_AVAILABLE:
        return best_a, best_m

    N = 2 * n - 1
    L = next_pow2(N)
    focus = max(10.0, float(focus))
    temp = max(1e-300, float(m0) / focus)
    scale = 1.0 / focus

    theta = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    theta = theta - float(np.mean(theta))
    theta = np.clip(theta, -50.0, 50.0)

    LBFGS_STATE["x"] = theta.copy()
    LBFGS_STATE["count"] = 0
    LBFGS_DEADLINE = deadline
    LBFGS_MAX_EVAL = max_eval

    try:
        res = fmin_l_bfgs_b(
            lbgf_fprime,
            theta,
            args=(n, L, temp, scale),
            maxiter=int(max_iter),
            ftol=1e-12,
            pgtol=1e-6,
            maxcor=20,
            maxls=20,
            callback=lbgf_callback,
            disp=0
        )
        theta_out = np.asarray(res[0], dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        if LBFGS_STATE["x"] is not None:
            theta_out = np.asarray(LBFGS_STATE["x"], dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            theta_out = theta

    a2 = log_to_a(theta_out)
    m2 = conv_full_max(a2)
    if np.isfinite(m2) and m2 < best_m:
        best_m = m2
        best_a = a2

    if verbose:
        print(f"    lbgf focus={focus:.0f} score={2.0*n*best_m:.8f}")

    return best_a, best_m


def interpolate_sequence(a, new_n):
    a = normalize_positive(a)
    old_n = a.size
    new_n = int(new_n)
    if new_n <= 0:
        return a.copy()
    if old_n == new_n:
        return a.copy()
    if old_n == 1:
        return np.full(new_n, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)

    xold = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    xnew = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    val = np.interp(xnew, xold, a)
    return normalize_positive(val)


def make_power_ramp(n, power=0.5):
    n = int(n)
    x = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    with np.errstate(under="ignore", over="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        a = x ** float(power)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 1.0)
    return normalize_positive(a)


def make_mild_ramp(n, c=0.5):
    n = int(n)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    a = 1.0 + float(c) * x
    return normalize_positive(a)


def make_random_shape(n, base, rng):
    base = normalize_positive(base)
    n = int(base.size)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    a = base.copy()
    for k in range(1, 7):
        f = float(rng.uniform(k, k + 2))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.08)) / float(k)
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        a = a * (1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + ph))
    return normalize_positive(a)


def propose_candidate():
    t_start = get_time()
    deadline = t_start + 1770.0

    best_a = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    # Load parent if available.
    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if p.ndim == 1 and p.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            pc = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if float(pc.sum()) > 1e-12:
                parent = normalize_positive(pc)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        best_a, _, best_score = local_score(parent)
        print(f"Parent: n={best_a.size}, local_score={best_score:.8f}")
    else:
        best_a = make_power_ramp(4200, 0.5)
        _, _, best_score = local_score(best_a)
        print(f"Fallback init: n={best_a.size}, local_score={best_score:.8f}")

    best_n = int(best_a.size)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(424242)

    # Quick interpolation-only length checks.
    if deadline - get_time() > 20.0:
        for factor in (0.8, 0.9, 1.1, 1.2):
            nn = int(round(best_n * factor))
            if 1500 <= nn <= 10000 and nn != best_n:
                a_try, _, sc_try = local_score(interpolate_sequence(best_a, nn))
                if sc_try <= best_score + 1e-12:
                    best_a = a_try
                    best_n = int(a_try.size)
                    if sc_try < best_score:
                        best_score = sc_try
                        print(f"Interp n={best_n}: score={best_score:.8f}")

    # Main smooth L_r Adam stages.
    plan = [
        (4, 90, 0.12),
        (16, 120, 0.10),
        (64, 120, 0.09),
        (256, 150, 0.08),
        (16, 80, 0.10),
        (512, 150, 0.07),
        (256, 120, 0.07),
        (1024, 120, 0.06),
    ]
    total_cap = 0.0
    for _, cap, _ in plan:
        total_cap += float(cap)

    if deadline - get_time() > 300.0:
        main_budget = min((deadline - get_time()) * 0.55, 850.0)
        for idx, (order, cap, lr) in enumerate(plan):
            rem = deadline - get_time()
            if rem < 25.0:
                break
            t = min(main_budget * (float(cap) / total_cap), float(cap), rem)
            d = get_time() + t
            if d - get_time() < 10.0:
                continue

            print(f"\nAdam order={order}, lr={lr:.3f}, t={d-get_time():.1f}s")
            a_out, _ = adam_refine(
                best_a, d, order, lr=lr,
                stall_limit=8000, seed=1000 + 17 * idx
            )
            a_out, _, sc_out = local_score(a_out)
            if sc_out <= best_score + 1e-10:
                best_a = a_out
                best_n = int(best_a.size)
                if sc_out < best_score:
                    best_score = sc_out
                    print(f"  new best after order {order}: {best_score:.8f}")

    # A few fast alternative starts, in case the parent basin is not global.
    if deadline - get_time() > 180.0:
        alt_inits = [
            make_power_ramp(best_n, 0.5),
            make_power_ramp(best_n, 1.0),
            make_power_ramp(best_n, 2.0),
            make_mild_ramp(best_n, 0.6),
            make_random_shape(best_n, best_a, rng),
        ]
        alt_budget = min((deadline - get_time()) * 0.12, 120.0)
        per = max(8.0, alt_budget / float(len(alt_inits)))
        for idx, init in enumerate(alt_inits):
            rem = deadline - get_time()
            if rem < 15.0:
                break
            d = get_time() + min(per, rem)
            a_out, _ = adam_refine(init, d, 128, lr=0.08, stall_limit=3000, seed=5000 + 31 * idx)
            a_out, _, sc_out = local_score(a_out)
            if sc_out < best_score:
                best_a = a_out
                best_n = int(best_a.size)
                best_score = sc_out
                print(f"  alternative start {idx} new best: {best_score:.8f}")

    # L-BFGS polish with progressively sharper log-sum-exp.
    if SCIPY_OPT_AVAILABLE and best_n <= 10000 and deadline - get_time() > 80.0:
        lbgf_budget = min((deadline - get_time()) * 0.14, 220.0)
        for idx, focus in enumerate((30.0, 100.0, 300.0)):
            rem = deadline - get_time()
            if rem < 20.0:
                break
            d = get_time() + min(80.0, lbgf_budget / 3.0, rem)
            print(f"\nL-BFGS focus={focus:.0f}")
            a_out, _ = lbgf_polish(best_a, d, focus=focus, max_iter=250, max_eval=25000, verbose=True)
            a_out, _, sc_out = local_score(a_out)
            if sc_out <= best_score + 1e-12:
                best_a = a_out
                best_n = int(best_a.size)
                if sc_out < best_score:
                    best_score = sc_out
                    print(f"  new best after L-BFGS {focus:.0f}: {best_score:.8f}")

    # Local exact pair-mass transfer refinement.
    if deadline - get_time() > 70.0:
        d = get_time() + min(180.0, (deadline - get_time()) * 0.12)
        print(f"\nPair refine budget={d-get_time():.1f}s")
        a_out, _ = pair_refine(best_a, d, seed=777, active_count=140,
                               topcand=14, max_pairs=50, stall_limit=90)
        a_out, _, sc_out = local_score(a_out)
        if sc_out <= best_score + 1e-12:
            best_a = a_out
            best_n = int(best_a.size)
            if sc_out < best_score:
                best_score = sc_out
                print(f"  new best after pair refine: {best_score:.8f}")

    # Multi-length search.
    lengths = []
    for factor in (0.75, 0.9, 1.15, 1.35, 1.6):
        nn = int(round(best_n * factor))
        if 1500 <= nn <= 10000:
            lengths.append(nn)
    lengths = sorted(set(lengths))

    if len(lengths) > 3:
        lengths = lengths[:3]

    if len(lengths) > 0 and deadline - get_time() > 140.0:
        for idx, nn in enumerate(lengths):
            rem = deadline - get_time()
            if rem < 90.0:
                break
            if nn == best_n:
                continue

            print(f"\nTry length n={nn}")
            a_try = interpolate_sequence(best_a, nn)
            a_try, _, sc_try = local_score(a_try)
            if sc_try < best_score:
                best_a = a_try
                best_n = nn
                best_score = sc_try
                print(f"  interpolated length {nn} immediately improved: {best_score:.8f}")

            t1 = min(70.0, (rem - 20.0) * 0.40)
            t2 = min(90.0, (rem - 20.0) * 0.50)
            if t1 > 20.0:
                d1 = get_time() + t1
                a_out, _ = adam_refine(a_try, d1, 64, lr=0.09, stall_limit=5000, seed=9000 + 13 * idx)
                a_try, _, sc_try = local_score(a_out)
                if sc_try <= (best_score if len(a_try) != best_n else best_score) + 1e-10:
                    if sc_try < best_score:
                        best_score = sc_try
                        best_a = a_try
                        best_n = int(best_a.size)
                        print(f"  length {nn} improved: {best_score:.8f}")
                    else:
                        if len(a_try) != best_n:
                            best_a = a_try
                            best_n = int(best_a.size)
                else:
                    a_try = a_out

            rem = deadline - get_time()
            if t2 > 20.0 and rem > 40.0:
                d2 = get_time() + t2
                a_out, _ = adam_refine(a_try, d2, 256, lr=0.07, stall_limit=5000, seed=9100 + 13 * idx)
                a_out, _, sc_out = local_score(a_out)
                if sc_out <= best_score + 1e-10:
                    best_a = a_out
                    best_n = int(best_a.size)
                    if sc_out < best_score:
                        best_score = sc_out
                        print(f"  length {nn} final improved: {best_score:.8f}")

    # Final polish on the current best.
    if deadline - get_time() > 40.0:
        rem = deadline - get_time()
        t1 = min(120.0, rem * 0.40)
        d1 = get_time() + t1
        a_out, _ = adam_refine(best_a, d1, 512, lr=0.05, stall_limit=9000, seed=31337)
        a_out, _, sc_out = local_score(a_out)
        if sc_out <= best_score + 1e-10:
            best_a = a_out
            best_score = min(best_score, sc_out)
            if sc_out < best_score:
                best_score = sc_out
                print(f"Final Adam improved: {best_score:.8f}")

        if SCIPY_OPT_AVAILABLE and best_n <= 10000 and deadline - get_time() > 30.0:
            rem = deadline - get_time()
            t2 = min(80.0, rem * 0.40)
            d2 = get_time() + t2
            a_out, _ = lbgf_polish(best_a, d2, focus=1000.0, max_iter=120,
                                   max_eval=12000, verbose=True)
            a_out, _, sc_out = local_score(a_out)
            if sc_out <= best_score + 1e-10:
                best_a = a_out
                best_score = min(best_score, sc_out)
                if sc_out < best_score:
                    best_score = sc_out
                    print(f"Final L-BFGS improved: {best_score:.8f}")

        if deadline - get_time() > 25.0:
            rem = deadline - get_time()
            t3 = min(60.0, rem * 0.50)
            d3 = get_time() + t3
            a_out, _ = pair_refine(best_a, d3, seed=88888, active_count=160,
                                   topcand=16, max_pairs=60, stall_limit=60)
            a_out, _, sc_out = local_score(a_out)
            if sc_out <= best_score + 1e-10:
                best_a = a_out
                best_score = min(best_score, sc_out)
                if sc_out < best_score:
                    best_score = sc_out
                    print(f"Final pair improved: {best_score:.8f}")

    # Final sanity normalization.
    best_a = normalize_positive(best_a)
    final_list = [float(x) for x in best_a]

    m_final = conv_full_max(best_a)
    print(f"\nFINAL local: n={len(final_list)}, max_conv={m_final:.10e}, "
          f"score={2.0*len(final_list)*m_final:.8f}, elapsed={get_time()-t_start:.1f}s")

    return final_list
```
